# After-Release Punchlist

Items intentionally deferred to **after go-live** — enhancements, non-blocking polish, and anything that can only be exercised/verified once we're live in Production. Sibling to `Admin-Menus-Punchlist.md` (`AM-`) and `Payment-Test-Punchlist.md` (`PL-`); those two stay scoped to the pre-release verification pass.

- **Item IDs:** `AR-###`, numbered oldest → newest (newest at the bottom). The `AR-` prefix keeps this queue distinct from `AM-` / `PL-` — no number collisions, and Todd can grep `AR-` for the post-launch backlog.
- **Each item** carries Ann's request and, where known, a Claude-verified root cause (code + DB) and clear **For Todd** directions.
- **Nothing here blocks go-live.** If an item turns out to be release-critical, promote it to `AM-`/`PL-` and note the move.

---

> ### 🚨 AR-038 — ✅ FIX VERIFIED BY ANN 08-26. One half still open: has anything actually been OPENED?
> **The exposure is closed — and now measured, not just reasoned about.** A med form is gated on whether the event's own registration profile collects one (`UploadedDocumentPolicy`), enforced server-side on both `by-registration` routes and binding Superuser too. Storage stays per-player **by ruling** (Todd, 08-25): the parent owns the document and re-uploads per event.
> **✅ VERIFIED PASSING (Ann, 08-26)** on Clara Scheets / All American Aim, and confirmed against the dev DB the same day: **11 of 1,095 jobs collect med forms, all ONE customer**; of the **28,212** registrations held by the **3,131** players who own a form, **23,610 (84%) sat on non-collecting jobs and are now refused**; **customers able to reach a given player's form drops 52 → 1.** Clara alone: **21 registrations across 7 customers, exactly 1 on a collecting job.**
> **↳ Ann's *"You do not have permission to access this resource"* toast. ⛔ CORRECTED 2026-08-26 — the earlier *"not a defect, and it will not appear on other sites"* answer was WRONG on every load-bearing claim. Todd reproduced it the same day as SuperUser on the Details tab. FIXED — `21e066f1`.**
> That answer assumed `skipErrorToast()` worked. **It did not.** `auth.interceptor.ts` handled 403 *before* it ever read `SKIP_GLOBAL_ERROR_TOAST`, and toasted unconditionally — so the opt-out was dead for **all 16 callers** that passed it, on the one status a probe endpoint most needs it for. Nor was the probe an occasional failure: `ResolveRegistrationAccessAsync` returned **403 for "this event collects no med form"**, which is **1,087 of 1,098 jobs**. The Details tab therefore toasted on **essentially every player fly-in on every non-UM site** — not once, not transiently, and requiring no backend restart. Ann could not reproduce it because she went looking while standing on a *collecting* job.
> **Fixed two ways.** The event gate now answers **404** (`RegistrationAccess.NotCollected`) instead of 403 — *"nothing here for this event"* is not *"you are forbidden"* — and the interceptor honours the skip flag on 403. Wrong-role, wrong-job and no-such-registration still return **403**. **No access widened.**
> **🔴 NOT FIXED ON THE LIVE SITE — Ann, 08-26: *"The toast portion is what I'm saying isn't fixed on the live site!"*** **The toast half of AR-038 is NOT closed.** (An earlier line here read this as a prod verification — that was my misreading of her report, corrected same day.) **The fix spans BOTH stacks** — backend (`MedFormController` 403→404) and frontend (interceptor skip-flag) — **so a partial deploy still toasts.** **🔑 EITHER HALF ALONE SILENCES THIS TOAST — so seeing it means NEITHER is deployed to prod.** Work it through: **new backend + old frontend** → the probe returns **404**, which falls past the 403 branch to the generic safety net at `auth.interceptor.ts:128`, and **that one has always honoured the skip flag** → silent. **Old backend + new frontend** → **403**, and the repaired 403 branch honours the flag → silent. **So a partial deploy cannot produce this toast.** (An earlier line here claimed it could — wrong.)
**⇒ The live site is almost certainly running a build from before `21e066f1`.** Prod ships from TSIC-PHOENIX separately and the fix landed 08-26. **This is a deploy gap, not a code gap.**
**🔬 The 30-second check that settles it** — on the Details tab with the Network tab open, find the `medform/by-registration/{id}` request: **403 → the fix is not on prod** (deploy it); **404 → the fix IS on prod**, and the toast is coming from a **different endpoint**, which that same Network tab will name. ⚠ Ruled out already: the fly-in's headshot is a plain `<img>` against statics (`registration-detail-panel.component.html:71-80`), not an interceptor call, so it cannot toast.
> **STILL OPEN and time-sensitive — `LogAccess` review.** Every read is logged, allowed or denied. **Check whether any cross-customer form was ever actually opened.** That is what separates a latent exposure from a live incident, and it drives what has to be disclosed to whom. **Nothing in the code fix answers this.**

Cause: `[reporting].[TournamentKeyAttributes-ALL]` ran a stray `select convert(varchar, @thisYear)` between the `QA Test:` sheet marker and the data — an unnamed single-column result set, which the Excel exporter took as the sheet's column definition, discarding the real ~22 names. **One line deleted; headers restored.** The dates Ann also reported missing were never missing — `EventStart`/`EventEnd` are columns B and C, unreadable only because there was no header row. Repo `scripts/12-...` now matches live. **Contract to remember (`scripts/11-...:22-28`): report procs emit PAIRED result sets — marker, then rows. Any extra single-column select silently eats the headers.** AR-014's heading-rename question stays open and separate. | ✅ FIXED |
| **AR-014** | ✅ **CLOSED (Todd, 08-24).** Dates were ALWAYS in the report (cols B/C) — buried under 261 blank rows because 70% of jobs had no event date and SQL sorts NULLs first. Proc constrained to `j.ExpiryAdmin > getdate()` (375→151 rows, blanks 261→50); applied to dev+prod 08-23, definition banked in `scripts/12-reporting-tournamentkeys-live-only.sql`. No deploy needed (`kind:'sp'`). Missing header row was a separate exporter defect — AR-029, fixed 08-24. **Heading RENAME declined by default**: the cryptic names are the literal config flags Chelsea QAs job builds against; sort stays NULLs-first because a blank date is a finding. **📣 ANSWER FOR ANN in the entry — she is told to return only if something needs changing in CODE.** | ✅ CLOSED |

If no job has ever had 2+ leagues, this is **unreachable** and should close as Won't Fix rather than stay parked. One query settles it: `SELECT JobId, COUNT(*) FROM Leagues GROUP BY JobId HAVING COUNT(*) > 1`. She has seen it and is not asking for a fix. | 🔵 close-out call |

### AR-039: [ARB Health] Four fixes — stale team on the table, no sort/export, drop "Defensive", and the send dialog lets you send twice
- **Topic**: ARB Subscription Health → Lookup Behind in Payment → table + email composer
- **Request (Ann, 08-25)**: four items, filed together because they are one screen. **Part 4 is the one to do first — it can double-charge a family's inbox.**

**1️⃣ The table shows the team the player was on when the SUBSCRIPTION was created, not their current team.**
- **Cause**: `ArbDefensiveService.cs:621` reads **`Assignment = reg.Assignment`** — a **denormalised text snapshot** stored on the registration row as `"Player: <team name>"`, not a live lookup.
- That string is re-written by *some* move paths (`PlayerRegistrationService.cs:405/450/459`, `ClubRosterService.cs:83`, `SelfRosterUpdateController.cs:140`) — **but evidently not all of them**, which is exactly how it goes stale. Chasing every writer is the wrong fix; there will always be one more.
- **Recommended fix: stop reading the snapshot. Derive the team at query time from `Registrations.AssignedTeamId` → `Teams.TeamName`.** That is the canonical live value and cannot drift. ⚠ Handle the null case (no assigned team) so the column blanks rather than showing a stale name.
- **⚠ Worth a wider look**: `reg.Assignment` is a stored display string, so **any other screen reading it has the same staleness risk.** Not part of this item, but worth knowing before someone reuses it.

**2️⃣ Make Lookup Behind in Payment sortable and exportable.**
- The table is **hand-rolled** — `arb-health.component.html:119`, `<table class="table table-sm table-hover">`. So neither sorting nor export exists to switch on; both must be built.
- **Recommend converting it to an ej2 grid instead of hand-rolling sort + an export**: sorting, column ordering and the **Excel toolbar button all come free**, and there is a fresh in-house precedent — the STP club-reps grid Todd built for AR-021 is exactly that shape.
- ⚠ The table carries the contact-card cell added under PL-056 (player/contact emails and phones as `mailto:`/`tel:` links). **Whatever replaces it must keep those**, and an Excel export should carry them as plain text columns.

**3️⃣ Remove "Defensive" from the wording.** Ann: *"it's offensive and not necessary."*
- **✅ Exactly ONE user-facing occurrence** — `arb-health.component.html:270`, **"Compose Defensive Email"** → **"Compose Email"**. That is the whole of the visible change.
- **⚠ The word is also the internal service name** (`ArbDefensiveService`, `IArbDefensiveService`, its file, and the `arbService` call sites). Ann said *"remove Defensive wherever it appears"*. **Decide whether that includes the code identifiers**: renaming the class is a safe mechanical refactor but touches many files and is invisible to users. **Recommend: change the label now, and treat the class rename as an optional tidy-up** — but say which was done, so nobody reads the item as half-finished.

**4️⃣ 🔴 After a successful send the dialog stays open with the recipients still selected — so it can be sent again.**
- **Confirmed at `arb-health.component.ts:313-321`**: on success it sets `sendResult`, clears `isSending`… **and leaves `showEmailDialog` true with the selection intact.** The Send button goes live again immediately.
- **⚠ The error path is the one that closes the dialog** (`:322-325`, `showEmailDialog.set(false)`). **That is backwards** — a failure hides the composer, a success invites a repeat.
- **This is a dunning email to families who owe money.** A second send is not a cosmetic annoyance; it reads as harassment and generates support calls. **Fix: on success, close the composer (or disable Send and clear the selection) and surface the result — sent/failed counts — outside the dialog.** Ann's wording: *"Need to correct this."*
- ⚠ Check the sibling path at `:349-360` (**"Send Warning to All"**) — it has the same shape and likely the same defect.
- **Severity**: 🔴 for part 4 (duplicate dunning emails to paying customers' families) · 🟡 part 1 (wrong team shown to a director chasing a payment) · 🟢 parts 2–3
- **Status**: 🔴 OPEN — for Todd. Filed 08-25 at Ann's request. **Do part 4 first.**

### AR-040: [Player Accounting fly-in] Show the subscription TOTAL on the ARB Subscription card
- **Topic**: Search Registrations → Player Accounting fly-in → **ARB Subscription** card
- **Request (Ann, 08-25)**: add **Subscription Total Amount** to the **second row, first position** — ahead of the installment Amount and the Occurrences count. Screenshot: Quinn Ahrenholz, YJN 2028 Howe, `$810.00 / every month × 6`.

**✅ The value already exists and is already being sent to the browser — it is simply never rendered.**
- `SubscriptionDetailDto.TotalAmount` (`AccountingDtos.cs:162`) is a **required** field, populated on **every** path that builds the card: `RegistrationSearchService.cs:505` (stored snapshot), `:946` (live ADN read), `TeamSearchService.cs:332`.
- The generated model already carries it — `SubscriptionDetailDto.ts:10`, `totalAmount: number`.
- **So: no backend change, no DTO change, and NO `2-Regenerate-API-Models.ps1` run.** This is markup plus a little CSS.

**Where to add it — three cards render this same block, all with the value in hand:**
| File | Line |
|:--|:--|
| `registration-detail-panel.component.html` (**Ann's screenshot**) | 593-600 |
| `team-detail-panel.component.html` | 296-297 |
| `family-payment.component.html` | 126-127 |
- **Recommend doing all three.** They are the same card; leaving two of them without the total is the kind of inconsistency that comes back as its own punchlist item.

**⚠ The layout will not do what she asked by itself.** `.sub-grid` is **`grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr`** (`registration-detail-panel.component.scss:611-615`). Dropping in a 5th field wraps it to **row 2 = Total | Amount, row 3 = Occurrences** — not the three-across second row in her request.
- **Suggested fix that keeps row 1 exactly as it looks today**: `repeat(6, 1fr)`, with Subscription ID and Start Date each spanning 3, and Total / Amount / Occurrences each spanning 2. Row 1 is pixel-identical; row 2 becomes clean thirds.
- Check the narrow-width collapse too — the fly-in already goes single-column on small screens, and three fields across is the case most likely to overflow. **Ann has now raised no-horizontal-scroll twice (AR-006, AR-016)** — don't hand her a third.

**⚠ Label it as the PLAN total, not the family's total.** `TotalAmount = perOccurrence × occurrences` — it is what the subscription will draft, which is **not** the registration's total fee whenever a deposit was paid up front at registration (that money sits outside the ARB plan). Ann's own words are *"Subscription Total Amount"*, which is right; keep "Subscription" in the label so nobody reads it as the balance.
- ⚠ Minor, and **not blocking**: none of the three computations is **ARB-Trial aware** (no `trialAmount` / `trialOccurrences` anywhere in these reads), so on a trial plan the trial occurrence would be priced at the recurring rate. **Checked the dev DB — `Jobs.adnARBTrial = 1` on ZERO jobs**, so this is theoretical today. Worth a comment at the computation rather than work.
- ### ✅ BUILT — Todd, 2026-08-26. Frontend only; commit `5853ac9c`.
  - **⚠ THE LABEL IS THE BUILD, NOT A CAVEAT — and the "not blocking" framing above understated it.** Measured on dev: **2,249 of 14,981 subscriptions differ from `fee_total` by more than $1, the worst by $4,680** (`173.33 × 3 = $519.99` plan against a `$5,200` fee; `450.00 × 9 = $4,050` against a `$0.00` fee). And this card renders **directly beneath the Fee/Paid/Owed ledger block** (`registration-detail-panel.component.html`, the ARB card immediately follows `app-accounting-ledger`). A bare "Total" there is a fourth money figure under three it contradicts by thousands. Shipped as **"Plan Total"** with an `aria-describedby` note: *"what the subscription drafts — money paid up front at registration is not part of the plan, so this is not the family's balance."*
  - **No backend change, no regen** — as filed. `TotalAmount` was already required and already populated. ⚠ Correction to the filing: there are **FIVE** construction sites, not three — `RegistrationRepository.cs:2651` and `TeamRepository.cs:1428` were missed. All five compute `perOccurrence × occurrences`, so nothing behavioural turns on it.
  - **Layout**: `repeat(6, minmax(0, 1fr))`, row 1 spans 3, row 2 spans 2 — Ann's three-across second row. Row 1 is genuinely pixel-identical (a span-3 track plus its 2 internal gaps equals one track of a 2-col grid). `minmax(0, …)` is the blowout guard, **not** the "minmax is not a cap" trap, which bites on a fixed max rather than `1fr`. **Added the single-column collapse at `767.98px` that never existed** — no `.sub-grid` media query was present in any of the three.
  - **⚠ THE CARD SCSS WAS TRIPLICATED AND HAD ALREADY DRIFTED.** `.sub-grid` / `.sub-field` / `.sub-label` / `.sub-value` / `.sub-stored-note` existed in all three components, and **only the team panel carried a wrap rule on `.sub-value`** — so a long subscription id could overflow the other two, including the panel Ann uses. Now one partial, `shared-ui/styles/_arb-subscription-card.scss`, `@use`d by all three, with `overflow-wrap: anywhere` everywhere. **Anyone adding a sixth field changes the grid once, not three times.**
  - **`family-payment` keys the note id on `card.regId`** — its card is inside an `@for` over subscribed registrations, so a static id would repeat.
  - **⛔ ARB-Trial deliberately NOT addressed, and the note above was too soft about why it is safe.** The trial path is fully implemented, not absent: `AdnApiService.cs:903-904/965-966` mints `totalOccurrences = 2, trialOccurrences = 1` with `trialAmount = deposit` and `amount = balance`, so a trial plan's true total is `deposit + balance` while all five sites compute `balance × 2`. **Verified directly: `Jobs.adnARBTrial = 1` on ZERO jobs AND zero teams carry a subscription** — and the trial path is team-only, so no row can reach it. Left alone on purpose; if team ARB Trial is ever switched on, the multiply is the thing to fix.
  - `ng build` clean; `verify:no-effect` passes. No backend build needed — nothing backend changed.
- **Severity**: 🟢 small · display-only · no money logic touched
- **Status**: 🟡 **BUILT — Todd, 08-26. Source-only, awaiting deploy.** Filed 08-25, built the next day.
  - **HOW TO VERIFY (Ann, locally)**: open a registrant with an ARB plan → the **ARB Subscription** card now reads **Subscription ID | Start Date** on row 1 and **Plan Total | Amount | Occurrences** on row 2, with the qualifier line beneath. Check the same card in the **family** fly-in (a sibling with a plan) and on a **team** detail panel. Narrow the window to confirm the card collapses to one column instead of scrolling sideways.
  - **⚠ Plan Total will NOT equal Owed on many registrants, and that is correct.** It is what the subscription drafts, not the balance — 15% of plans differ from the registration fee. If it looks wrong, read the qualifier line before filing.

### AR-041: ["Invite all Players" button] Not the filed bug — but the label hides that it's a CROSS-EVENT invite, and the token flag it's supposed to serve has never been switched on anywhere
- **Topic**: Search Registrations → results toolbar → **Invite all Players**
- **Filed claim (Ann, 08-25)**: the button shows when (1) Player is the selected role filter and (2) **Require Invitation Token** on Configure → Job Settings → Player tab is **NOT** checked. *"It shows on as many sites as I checked so this is a bug."*

**⚠ The stated cause is wrong, and the correction matters — otherwise this gets "fixed" into something worse.**
- **The current job's token flag is not part of the gate, and shouldn't be.** The button does **not** invite players into the event you're looking at — they're already registered on it. It invites them into **a different event of the same customer**, chosen from a target-job picker inside the modal (`batch-email-modal.component.html:134-145`), which is **required before sending** (`:340`). The dialog title is *"Send Invitation Batch Email."*
- **The real gate** (`search-registrations.component.ts:1112-1118`): exactly **one** role filter = Player **AND** `eligiblePlayerInviteTargetJobs.length > 0`. Targets come from `JobRepository.cs:548-558` — same customer, not the current job, `ExpiryUsers` not passed, `bRegistrationAllowPlayer = 1`.
- **The token flag is skipped ON PURPOSE, and it's commented** (`JobRepository.cs:554-555`): *"Only the accept-registration flag gates the target — the requires-token flag is intentionally NOT checked (an invite is valid into an open-enrollment event too)."* That rationale is sound: `!INVITE_LINK` still produces a working pre-filled registration link when the target is open enrollment.

**✅ But Ann's observation is exactly right, and the DB says something bigger than the filing did.** Measured on dev 08-25:
| Measure | Count |
|:--|--:|
| Live jobs | 154 |
| Live jobs where the button SHOWS (≥1 sibling target) | **129** |
| …of those, having ≥1 target that REQUIRES a player token | **0** |
| **ALL 1,095 jobs ever — `BPlayerRegRequiresToken = 1`** | **0** |
| **ALL 1,095 jobs ever — `BTeamRegRequiresToken = 1`** | **0** |
- **Neither invite-token flag has ever been turned on, on any job, player or team.** So `!INVITE_LINK` is currently minting tokens that gate nothing, everywhere — and the button appears on **129 of 154 live jobs**, which is precisely the *"shows on as many sites as I checked"* Ann saw. Her instinct was right; the mechanism was the current job's checkbox rather than the design decision above.

- **Status**: 🅿 **PARKED — Todd, 08-26.** *"Why would I remove a fantastic new feature like that?"* Nothing to build; loses to the current queue. **Do not remove the button.**

⛔ **SUPERSEDED 08-26 — THE EVIDENCE BELOW IS INVALID. READ THIS FIRST.**
- **The "never used" statistics are a recency artifact.** Git dates: `!INVITE_LINK` player invites **2026-03-16** (`5c9ce385`), club-rep **03-17** (`4305963a`), and the invite-mode batch-email UX — the button, guidance panel and settings fieldset — **2026-07-03** (`5c766e3f`). **Go-live was 08-08/09.** The feature has been in front of real directors for **18 days.**
- So "0 invite-link sends out of 24,662 logged emails" measures a log that is overwhelmingly **legacy-era and pre-golive** against a feature that has existed in the UI for two and a half weeks and requires a director to deliberately set up invitation-only registration on a *future* event. It does not say "never used." It says **"not used yet"** — the unremarkable state of any new feature.
- Same defect in `BPlayerRegRequiresToken = 1` on **0 of 1,095 jobs**: the column did not exist before **2026-03-16**, and most of those 1,095 jobs predate it. The counts are accurate; the inference drawn from them was not.
- ⚠ **My relabel recommendation was abandoned on this bad inference — it is reinstated as the standing recommendation.** Relabel to **"Invite Players to Another Event…"**, which answers the real confusion; if it still reads as too prominent, demote it to the overflow menu. Reversible — removal is not.
- **What survives the correction:** Ann's underlying complaint is sound and still unaddressed. The button does show on **129 of 154 live jobs**, and *"Invite all Players"* reads as *invite these players* when it means *invite these players into a **different** event*.
- ⚠ **Ann ruled REMOVE on the invalid statistics below. She must re-decide with these dates in front of her before anything is built.**

**✅ RULED — Ann, 08-25: *"I don't agree with AR-041. It shouldn't be visible."* REMOVE THE BUTTON.**
- My first recommendation was to **relabel** it ("Invite Players to Another Event…") and keep it, on the reasoning that it fronts a working cross-event feature. **Ann overruled that, and a further check proves she is right and I was wrong** — the feature is not merely under-used, **it has never been used at all.**

**📊 ~~THE EVIDENCE THAT DECIDES IT~~ (dev DB, 08-25) — ⛔ IT DECIDES NOTHING; see the correction above. The counts are accurate, the inference is not:**
| Measure | Count |
|:--|--:|
| Emails ever logged | 24,662 |
| **Invite-link emails EVER sent (`?invite=` in body)** | **0** |
| …"register by invitation as Player" / "…as Club Rep" | **0 / 0** |
| Unconfigured `[INVITE LINK — …]` placeholders leaked into a send | 0 |
| Jobs ever setting `BPlayerRegRequiresToken` / `BTeamRegRequiresToken` | **0 / 0** (of 1,095) |
| Live jobs showing the button | **129 of 154** |
- **So the button appears on 129 of 154 live jobs to front a feature that has never once been used, whose token requirement has never been enabled anywhere.** That is pure noise on almost every site, and it is exactly the kind of thing that generates reports like this one.
- ⚠ **Do NOT mistake the "Invite" emails in the log for usage.** STEPS Elite AIM sent a run of them in July 2026 (*"STEPS Elite AIM - 2032 Invite"* etc.) — checked, and **none carries a token link.** They are ordinary batch emails using "Invite" as club language.

**⛔ FALSE — corrected 08-26: REMOVAL WOULD DELETE THE CAPABILITY, not just the button.** The target-event picker, expiry dropdown and staging test inbox all sit inside `@if (inviteMode())` (`batch-email-modal.component.html:111`), and the toolbar action is the only thing that sets `inviteMode`. With it gone, typing `!INVITE_LINK` flips `requiresInviteLink()` → `canSend()` false → the `:340` guard toasts *"Select a target registration event"* — **and no picker ever renders.** Dead end. `availableTokens` (`:153-155`) also deliberately omits the invite tokens (*"seeded by the Invite action"*), so nothing in the UI would even mention the token exists. The original claim, retained to show what was wrong with it — it cited the two **guards** (`:196`, `:340`, which do fire on `inviteMode() || requiresInviteLink()`) and mistook them for the **picker**: `requiresInviteLink()` (`batch-email-modal.component.ts:163-166`) detects `!INVITE_LINK` / `!CLUBREP_INVITE_LINK` typed into **any** batch-email body, and the target-event picker and its send guard fire on `inviteMode() || requiresInviteLink()` (`:197`, `:340`). **So a director who ever wants an invite can put the token in a normal Email All / Email Selected and get the identical flow.**

**🎯 ~~BUILD~~ — ⛔ SUSPENDED 08-26, PARKED. Nothing is to be built until Ann re-decides on the corrected dates; and if it is ever revived, it must also un-gate the picker (item 4 above) or the capability goes with the button.** Original spec retained for reference: remove the **Invite** toolbar action (both the Player and Club Rep variants) from the Search Registrations results toolbar. **Leave everything else alone** — the modal's invite plumbing, `TextSubstitutionService`'s `!INVITE_LINK` substitution, `GetInviteTargetJobsForCustomerAsync`, and the two Configure checkboxes all stay, so nothing is destroyed and the path above keeps working.
- ⚠ **Do not implement this by gating on the token flag** — same visible result, but it would leave a button that silently reappears the moment anyone ticks that box, which is the confusing behaviour we are removing.
- 🔸 **Worth a separate decision later** (not part of this item): the two **Require Invitation Token** checkboxes on the Player and Teams tabs configure a feature nobody has ever used. Either they should be exercised or they are inviting confusion of their own.
- **Severity**: 🟢 remove one toolbar action · no backend change · nothing lost
- **Status**: 🔴 OPEN — for Todd. Filed 08-25, **ruled by Ann the same day.** The original "relabel it" recommendation is **superseded — do not build it.**

### AR-042: [Add Accounting Record] Require the check number — Check is the one path that posts money in a single click, and it can't be fully undone
- **Topic**: Payment Ledger → **+ Add Accounting Record** → **Check** tab (`accounting-ledger.component.ts`)
- **Raised by Chelsea via Ann (08-25)**: people will record checks by mistake because **Check is the default tab**.

**✅ CONFIRMED, and the hazard is sharper than the concern stated.**
- `beginNormalEntry()` (`:499-502`) sets `paymentType = 'check'` **and pre-fills Amount with the full balance due**.
- `canSubmitPayment()` (`:600-602`) lets Check submit on **`amt > 0 && amt <= checkBalanceDue()` and nothing else — the check number is never inspected.** Compare the CC branch (`:595-599`), which demands **five** fields (number, expiry, CVV, first, last).
- **Net: Check is the only route from "dialog opened by accident" to "money posted", in one click, with the amount already filled in.** We currently require five fields to charge a card and **zero** to record a check — backwards, because the check is the one that can't be cleanly reversed.

**⚠ AND IT IS NOT CLEANLY REVERSIBLE — this is the strongest fact in the item.**
- Recording a check sets **`reg.BActive = true`** (`RegistrationSearchService.cs:836-841`), which is what *"puts them onto rosters/coach views/division counts"* (its own comment).
- **A Correction does NOT reverse it.** Same comment: *"Corrections are fee adjustments, not tender, so they do NOT activate"* — and there is **no `BActive = false` on any correction or refund path.** So undoing a mis-keyed check fixes the dollars and **leaves the player sitting on the roster and in the division counts.**

**🎯 DECIDED — Ann, 08-25. Build exactly this and no more:**
1. **Require a non-empty check number before "Record Check" will fire.** Add `checkNo` to the check branch of `canSubmitPayment()`. Ann: *"requiring a check number to prevent one-click entry and force thought on whether this is the option desired… Check number validation should be enough."*
2. **⛔ NO confirmation step. Explicitly declined by Ann** — *"I don't like adding an additional step (too many second steps overall that users complain about)."* **Do not add one**, even though the CC path has `showCcConfirm` and Check has none. The field-level guard is the whole fix.

**Notes for the build:**
- **One component only.** The guard lives in `accounting-ledger.component.ts`; `family-payment` has no second copy — it hands the event off (`family-payment.component.ts:439-444`).
- ⚠ **Do NOT make `CheckNo` required on the DTO.** It's `string?` across `AccountingDtos.cs` and **Corrections legitimately have none** — the guard must be conditional on the check branch, not a contract change.
- Mirror it server-side in the check branch (`RegistrationSearchService.cs:797`, `:566`); a frontend-only guard is bypassable, and this one protects money.
- **Costs legitimate users essentially nothing** — dev DB: of **62** check rows, only **5** have a blank check number and **none in the last 90 days**. (⚠ dev is a stale prod backup, so treat as directional.) An admin recording a real check is holding it.

**Still open, not decided:**
- **Chelsea's option 2** — move the three type buttons onto the Payment Ledger card to save a step. **Not ruled on.** It was rejected as a *safety* measure (it shortens the accidental path), but **with the check-number guard in place it is no longer a safety question**, and it points the same way as Ann's dislike of extra steps. Chelsea's option 1 (CC as default) is superseded and can be dropped.
- ~~Should reversing a check also clear `BActive`?~~ **✅ RULED — Ann, 08-25: NO. *"Reversing a check shouldn't change a status."*** **Current behaviour is CORRECT and deliberate — leave it.** A correction is a fee adjustment, not tender; it must not silently pull a player off rosters, coach views and division counts as a side effect of a bookkeeping entry.
  - **This ruling is safe because status is separately controllable**: the fly-in already has a manual active/inactive toggle (`registration-detail-panel.component.ts:1183-1189` → `searchService.setActive`). So an admin who truly needs the status changed does it as its own deliberate act — which is the right shape.
  - ⚠ Do **not** add a warning about it either. Combined with the no-confirmation-step ruling above, **the check-number guard is the entire fix for AR-042.**
- **Severity**: 🟡 one-click posting of unreversible money-plus-roster state · fix is a few lines
- **Status**: 🔴 OPEN — for Todd. Filed 08-25.

### AR-043: [Payments / ARB] Team-level ARB has no liveness rule anywhere — LATENT, and armed the day `adnARBTrial` is switched on for any job
- **Topic**: club-rep "Pay Balance Due" nudge + the Team Registration payment step · the club-rep half split out of **AR-013** (Claude, 08-26)
- **⛔ NOTHING TO BUILD TODAY — and that is the whole point of the filing.** Both defects below are real in code and **impossible for any customer to reach**, because nothing in the system has ever created a team-level ARB subscription. File-and-wait, not build.
- **📊 MEASURED 08-26 (`TSICV5`, `(local)\SS2016` — same dataset AR-013 was decided on; its registration-side counts still reproduce exactly: `expired` = 4,581 with 59 owing):**
  - `Leagues.teams WHERE AdnSubscriptionId IS NOT NULL` → **0 rows.** No team has ever held an ARB plan id.
  - `Jobs.Jobs` → **`adnARBTrial` = 0 of 1,098 jobs.** (`adnARB`, the registration-level flag, is on for 50 — that is the player side, already fixed under AR-013.)
  - `PaymentService.cs:698-703` (the ArbTrial deposit/balance schedule) is the **only** path that writes `team.AdnSubscriptionId`. It is fully built and unit-tested (`ArbTrialTeamPaymentServiceTests`), just never enabled.
- **⚠ THE PRECONDITION — this is what the item exists to record.** Enabling `adnARBTrial` on a job is a **config change a SuperUser can make without touching code**, and the first team that enrolls arms **both** defects at once. If that flag is ever turned on, **these ship first.**

**1️⃣ The club-rep nudge gates on id-presence, not liveness — the AR-013 bug class, club-rep side.**
- `JobRepository.cs:1077-1079` computes the rep's payable balance as `.Where(t => t.AdnSubscriptionId == null)`. The id is never cleared when a plan ends (`TeamSearchService.cs:371` writes `AdnSubscriptionStatus = "canceled"` and leaves the id), so once a team has ever drafted, its balance drops out of the sum **permanently** — and all three consumers gate on `> 0`, so they go dark together: `client-header-bar.component.ts:123`, `registration-panel.component.ts:274`, `smart-bulletins.component.ts:143` (the last is the panel's mount gate).
- **The suppression itself is correct and must stay** — an ARB team's `OwedTotal` stays positive while the plan drips, and summing it would nag a fully-ARB rep forever. Same as AR-013: right concept, wrong condition.
- **Remedy — two lines, one file, no DTO change, NO `2-Regenerate-API-Models.ps1`.** `ClubRepNonArbOwed` → `MyClubRepNonArbOwed` already flows through `JobsController.cs:316`; only the predicate moves:
  ```csharp
  .Select(t => new { t.OwedTotal, t.ViPolicyId, t.AdnSubscriptionId, t.AdnSubscriptionStatus })
  ...
  ClubRepNonArbOwed = teams
      .Where(t => !ArbSubscriptionStatus.IsLive(t.AdnSubscriptionId, t.AdnSubscriptionStatus))
      .Sum(t => t.OwedTotal ?? 0m),
  ```
  The query already `ToListAsync`es before summing, so the helper needs no EF translation — the client-side-compute workaround the player-side fix required does not even arise. `IsLive` anchors on the id, so a team with **no** plan reads *not live* and stays payable: ordinary non-ARB teams are unaffected.
- **Today `ClubRepNonArbOwed` ≡ `ClubRepTotalOwed`** for every rep in the system — the filter excludes nothing. The explanatory comment at `JobRepository.cs:1074-1076` describes a hazard that has never occurred.

**2️⃣ 🔴 The bigger half: the Team Registration payment path has NO ARB concept at all.** *(This is the one that would cost money on enablement.)*
- The pulse flag is only a **nudge gate**; the destination page recomputes independently — and nothing on that path filters ARB teams, client **or** server:
  - `team-payment.service.ts:162-195` maps **every** team; `balanceDue` / `totalCcOwed` sum every `owedTotal`; `teamIdsWithBalance` filters only on `> 0`.
  - `RegisteredTeamShaper.cs` — the server-side builder of those per-team totals — contains **no** `AdnSubscription` or `Arb` reference whatsoever.
  - `PartitionArbEnrolled` / `ArbSubscriptionStatus.IsLive`, the write-side double-charge intercept, takes `List<Registrations>` (`PaymentService.cs:1881`). **Registrations only. The team payment path never calls it.**
- **So once team ARB is live**, a rep with one drafting team plus one ordinary owing team lands on a page that charges the drafting team's balance by hand as well — the exact double payment `PartitionArbEnrolled` exists to prevent, one layer down. **A real build**, not a predicate swap: the team path needs its own partition, and the shaper needs to carry the flag.
- **⚠ Fix order if this ever arms: 2️⃣ before 1️⃣.** 1️⃣ strands a rep who *cannot pay*; 2️⃣ charges one *twice*.
- **Severity**: latent — zero instances, zero reachable jobs, no money at risk in the current configuration. Would be 🔴 on the day `adnARBTrial` is enabled.
- **Status**: 🟡 **FILED 08-26 (Claude), NOT FOR BUILD.** Revisit only if `adnARBTrial` is turned on for a job, or if a `Leagues.teams` row ever shows a non-null `AdnSubscriptionId`. Both queries above are the check.

### AR-044: [USA Lacrosse Reconciliation] "Age Verif..." reads **No** for everyone — we compare USA Lacrosse's answer to the wrong word
- **Topic**: USA Lacrosse menu → **USA Lacrosse Membership Reconciliation** → *Age Verif…* column (`uslax-membership.component.ts`)
- **Request (Ann, 08-26)**: a **STEPS Lacrosse director** asks why Age Verification says **No**, when any player who renewed their membership completed it with USA Lacrosse. *"Need to determine how this field is verified with USA Lacrosse and expect most if not all players will say YES."*

**✅ CONFIRMED — a one-line display bug, and the evidence is already in this repo.**
- `ageVerifiedDisplay()` (`:442-446`) renders **Yes** only when the value lowercases to the literal **`'true'`**; **every other non-null value falls through to `No`.**
- **USA Lacrosse does not send `true`. It sends `"Approved"`.** Proven by a **captured live response** committed as a test fixture — `UsLaxServiceBatchTests.cs:255-280`, comment: *"Confirmed by debugger capture"*, real membership IDs (`000005374328`, `000005540585`), `"age_verified": "Approved"` on both records.
- `"Approved"` ≠ `"true"` → **No**, for every verified player. The director is right.
- ⚠ Note the value is **not null** for these rows — null renders as **blank** (`:444`), not "No". A screen full of "No" is therefore proof that USA Lacrosse **is** answering and we are mis-reading the answer.

**⚠ THE SYSTEM ALREADY CONTRADICTS ITSELF ON THIS FIELD.** The email token **`!USLAXAGEVERIFIED`** (`UsLaxMembershipService.cs:324`) substitutes the **raw** value — so a player whose grid row says **No** receives an email saying **Approved**. One data point, two answers, depending on the surface.
- **Legacy showed the raw string too** (`reference/.../USLaxMembershipController.cs:502`, `age_verified ?? "Unknown"`). It never reduced this to Yes/No — which is exactly why a long-time STEPS director spotted the new readout as wrong.

**🎯 FIX — do NOT just add `"approved"` to the comparison.** The real defect is collapsing a vocabulary we don't know into a boolean, with **No as the default for anything unrecognised**. We have confirmed exactly one token (`Approved`); *Pending*, *Denied*, *Not Required* and empty are all plausible and none is documented.
- **Recommend: show the raw value, as legacy did** — or map known tokens and **pass unknown values through verbatim rather than defaulting to No.** A blank or an unfamiliar word invites a question; a confident wrong **No** does not.
- ⚠ **The Excel export carries the same wrong value** — `onExcelQueryCellInfo` (`:476`) calls the same function, so exports handed to directors are wrong too. Fix once, both surfaces follow.
- 🔸 Defensive, while in there: `UsLaxService.cs:276` reads the field with `av.GetString()`, which **throws** if USA Lacrosse ever returns `age_verified` as a JSON boolean rather than a string. Harmless today, a hard failure the day their API changes shape.

**✅ SCOPE — display only. Nothing downstream is wrong.**
- `needsAction()` (`:382-394`) keys on `statusCode`, `memStatus` and expiry — **it never reads `ageVerified`**. So no row was wrongly flagged, **nobody was wrongly emailed**, and the reconciliation itself is sound (Ann's run: *199 checked, 195 expiry dates updated*).
- ⚠ **Set the director's expectation carefully**: the column should show **the truth**, not all-Yes. Once it reads USA Lacrosse's actual answer, most renewed players should show verified — but any genuinely unverified player must still show as such.
- **Severity**: 🟡 False readout on a compliance field shown to directors and exported to Excel — no wrong actions taken
- **Status**: 🔴 OPEN — for Todd. Filed 08-26.

### AR-045: [ARB Sweep] Stop the automatic family email on failed ARB drafts — keep the 2nd/15th expiring-card pass
- **Topic**: nightly ADN sweep → **step 8, family notify** (`AdnSweepService.cs:398-440`)
- **Request (Ann, 08-26)**: *"When ARB Sweep occurs request that the email to families on Failed ARB drafts NOT be sent each day. They already receive a failed transaction from ADN and Chelsea/directors want to review these cases before asking for payment. The tool in ARB Health works. Request is that automatic emails for expired credit cards on the 2nd and 15th of the month remain in effect."*

**✅ THE TWO SENDS ARE ALREADY CLEANLY SEPARATE — no risk of killing the wrong one.**
| Send | Where | Ann's ruling |
|:--|:--|:--|
| **Failed-draft family email** | `AdnSweepService.cs:433`, `NotifyFailedDraftsAsync`, inside every nightly sweep | 🔴 **STOP** |
| **Expiring-card family email** | `AdnSweepBackgroundService.cs:154-165`, `NotifyExpiringCardsAsync`, gated on `Day is 2 or 15` | ✅ **KEEP — untouched** |
| Manual send | ARB Health → Compose Email | ✅ **KEEP — Ann: *"The tool in ARB Health works."*** |

**🎯 RECOMMENDED SHAPE: a config toggle, not a deletion.** Add `EmailFailedDraftFamilies` to `AdnSweepOptions` (**default `false`** per this request), mirroring the existing `EmailMonthEndClose` precedent — *"Off switch for the unattended close only — the daily sweep keeps running either way."* Same idea here: the sweep still imports, still books money, still counts; only the outbound family mail stops. Reversible without a code change if Chelsea ever wants it back.

**⚠ TWO THINGS THAT MUST NOT BREAK WITH IT:**
1. **The digest must keep COUNTING failed drafts.** `FailedDraftsFound` / `FailedDraftsEmailed` / `FailedDraftsNotEmailed` ride the sweep result (`AdnSweepService.cs:494-496`), and `IAdnSweepService.cs:88` already defines the gap as *"FailedDraftsFound minus FailedDraftsEmailed is the number a human still has to contact."* **With the toggle off, every failed draft correctly lands in that bucket — which IS Ann's intent.** Do not let the toggle suppress the count as well as the send; that would take away the very visibility Chelsea reviews from.
2. **Do not touch the 2nd/15th pass or its no-dedupe ruling** (Todd, 08-25, comment at `AdnSweepBackgroundService.cs:143-153`). Ann has explicitly asked for that one to stay exactly as it is.

**⚠ THIS PARTLY REVERSES AR-037'S REMEDY — flag it rather than let it read as a regression.** AR-037 documented **$53,856 uncollectable across 30 dead plans**, with the mechanism finding that *"a correct import reaches nobody"* — **67 of 81 failures invisible, no family or director ever notified.** The sweep refactor answered that by notifying families.
- **Ann's new information changes the premise, not the finding: ADN already emails the family directly on a failed transaction.** So the family half was never actually silent — our email was a **duplicate** of ADN's, arriving while Chelsea was still deciding whether the charge should be chased at all.
- **What AR-037 identified that remains true is DIRECTOR visibility**, and that is preserved here — the digest count plus the ARB Health worklist. **Todd should confirm he agrees the AR-037 gap stays closed by digest + tool alone** before this is built; if he doesn't, this becomes a trade rather than a straight win.

**⚠ ONE CORRECTION TO THE FILING'S PREMISE — it is not sent "each day" for the same failure.** Step 8's comment documents exactly-once by construction: *"ImportArbTransactionAsync returns null for a transaction already in RegistrationAccounting, so an already-imported failure never reaches arbRows and cannot be emailed twice. A manual re-run the same morning re-mails nobody."*
- **But ADN RETRIES a failed draft, and each retry is a NEW transaction** — a new import, so a new email. **That is almost certainly what is being seen as "each day": one per retry attempt, not one per day per failure.** It does not change the request; it does mean the volume is per-retry, and that turning this off removes a *series* of duplicate nudges rather than a single one.
- **Severity**: 🟡 Behaviour change requested by the business — families currently receive a duplicate of ADN's own failure notice before a human has reviewed it
- **Status**: 🔴 OPEN — for Todd. Filed 08-26. **Needs his sign-off on the AR-037 interaction, then it is a small, reversible change.**

### AR-046: [TSIC-Teams app] Hide the Texting function — delivery is unverifiable BY CONSTRUCTION, not by omission
- **Topic**: **TSIC-Teams** mobile app → team **Texting**
- **Request (Ann, 08-26)**: on the phone *"only a certain number of phone numbers appear in the text header depending on phone type"*; 20 players plus parents means ~60 texts and *"all of them may or may not be sent… an issue with little way to verify."* **Recommends hiding/inactivating the text function.**

**✅ CONFIRMED — and the confirming fact is an absence.** **There is NO server-side SMS anywhere in this stack.** Searched the whole backend for a provider or sender — **no Twilio, no AWS SNS, no `SmsService`, no `ISmsSender`, nothing.** Every other outbound channel we own (registration mail, batch email, ARB notices, the sweep digest) runs through our own services and lands in `Jobs.emailLogs`. **Texting has no equivalent because we never send it.**
- **⇒ The app must be handing the message to the phone's own messaging app** (an `sms:` / `smsto:` hand-off). That single fact explains **every** symptom Ann reports, exactly:
  | Symptom | Cause |
  |:--|:--|
  | *"only a certain number of phone numbers appear… depending on phone type"* | iOS Messages, Android SMS and RCS each cap group recipients differently, and the **recipient delimiter differs by platform** (`,` vs `;`) — so numbers past the cap, or after a delimiter the OS doesn't accept, are **silently dropped from the header** |
  | *"may or may not be sent"* | Once the OS composer opens, sending is the **user's and the carrier's** act, not ours |
  | *"little way to verify"* | **We have no record because we never had the message.** There is nothing to log |

**🔴 THE DECIDING POINT: verification here is not a missing feature, it is structurally impossible.** After the hand-off the app cannot observe what the messaging app did — not how many recipients survived, not whether Send was pressed, not what the carrier delivered. **So this cannot be fixed by adding logging or a delivery report.** A coach texting 60 people has no way to know who got it, and neither do we when they ask. **Ann's recommendation is the right call.**

**🎯 RECOMMEND: hide it, as asked.**
- ⚠ **The change is NOT in this repo.** `reference/TSIC-TEAMS-2025` is an **empty directory** — the TSIC-Teams app lives in its own codebase (cf. AR-019, fixed *"in the TSIC-TEAMS rewrite"*). **This item is for Todd, but the edit lands elsewhere.**
- ⚠ **No per-job toggle exists to hide it with.** The mobile flags on `Jobs` are `bAllowMobileLogin`, `bAllowMobileRegn`, `bEnableTSICTeams`, `bEnableMobileRsvp`, `bEnableMobileTeamChat`, `mobileJobName`, `MobileScoreHoursPastGameEligible` — **nothing for texting.** So either **remove/hide it in the app** (simplest, matches Ann's *"on this app"*, no config, no migration) **or** add a `bEnableMobileTexting` flag if per-job control is ever wanted. **Recommend the app-side removal.**

**📊 BLAST RADIUS IS SMALL — but confirm before relying on it.** Of **154** live jobs on dev: **`bEnableTSICTeams` = 1**, `bEnableMobileRsvp` = 1, **`bEnableMobileTeamChat` = 0**.
- ⚠ **Dev is a stale prod backup — do not treat these as production adoption.** Re-run against prod before calling this low-impact.
- ⚠ **Team Chat is NOT a fallback today — zero jobs have it enabled.** So removing texting leaves **email** (Search Registrations → Email All, which *is* logged and verifiable) as the broadcast channel that works. **Say that plainly to the affected coaches rather than letting them discover it.**
- 🔸 **If real texting is ever wanted, it is a project, not a fix**: a server-side provider (Twilio/SNS), **10DLC brand + campaign registration**, opt-in capture and STOP/HELP handling, and per-message cost. The upside is that it would be logged and verifiable like everything else we send. **Not proposed here — flagged so the trade-off is on the record.**
- **Severity**: 🟡 Feature that cannot do what users assume it does, with no path to verification — recommend withdrawal rather than repair
- **Status**: 🔴 OPEN — for Todd. Filed 08-26 at Ann's recommendation. **First of her TSIC-Teams items.**

### AR-047: [TSIC-Teams calendar] Restore event Details/Notes · re-test "add an event" · refresh button beside Subscribe
- **Topic**: **TSIC-Teams** mobile app → Calendar
- **Request (Ann, 08-26)**, three parts:
  1. *"Recommend reverting to Google item format as features such as Notes (Details) are used."*
  2. *"Add an event to calendar… this function wasn't working previously, is it possible to see if this works."*
  3. *"Subscription for entire calendar works. Requesting a refresh button next to this option like there was in the prior version."*

**⚠ ALL THREE ARE APP-SIDE AND I CANNOT TEST THEM FROM HERE.** `reference/TSIC-TEAMS-2025` is an **empty directory** — the app has its own codebase (cf. AR-019, AR-046). **Part 2 in particular is a request to test on a device; nothing in this repo can answer it.** What follows is what the backend *does* own, so Todd starts from facts rather than re-deriving them.

**🔎 WHAT THIS BACKEND ACTUALLY SERVES — and what it doesn't:**
- **The ONLY calendar output in the entire stack is the web Schedule report export** — `ReportingController.ScheduleExportIcal:377` → `ReportingService.ExportScheduleToICalAsync:845-876`. It is a **POST with an explicit list of game IDs returning a one-shot `.ics` file.**
- **There is NO calendar SUBSCRIPTION feed here — and none in legacy either.** Searched both; legacy has exactly the same single export (`reference/.../ReportingController.cs:888`). **So the subscription Ann says works is served from the app or from something outside both codebases. Locate it before designing part 3.**

**🔴 A REAL REGRESSION I CAN NAME — the new ICS DROPPED `DESCRIPTION` entirely.** Legacy emitted `DESCRIPTION:{jobName}` on every event; **the rewrite emits no `DESCRIPTION` line at all.** If any part of Ann's "Notes (Details)" complaint touches this export, **that is the cause and it is a one-line restore.**
- ⚠ **Honest limit: legacy's DESCRIPTION was only the job name, repeated on every event — it was never per-game "Notes".** So restoring it verbatim would not give her what she is describing. **What "Notes (Details)" should contain is a question for Ann** — most likely the game/event note field, which means the ICS builder needs it in `ScheduleGameForICalDto` first.

**🔸 TWO SPEC DEFECTS IN THE SAME EXPORT — candidates for part 2, if that path uses it:**
1. **No `DTSTAMP`.** RFC 5545 makes it **REQUIRED** in a `VEVENT`. Neither version emits it, and **Google Calendar is notably strict about it** — a plausible reason an "add to calendar" silently fails. **One line.**
2. **Unescaped commas in `LOCATION`** — `string.Join(", ", …)` writes raw commas into a TEXT value where RFC 5545 requires `\,`. **Legacy escaped them correctly** (`"LOCATION:{0}\\, {1}\\, …"`), so this is a second quiet regression; some clients truncate the location at the first comma.
- ⚠ **These are candidates, not a diagnosis.** I cannot confirm the app's "add an event" uses this endpoint — it may build the event natively. **Don't let a plausible-looking fix here substitute for actually testing part 2 on a device.**

**⚠ PART 3 NEEDS SCOPING BEFORE IT IS BUILT — a "refresh" on a true subscription may not mean what it sounds like.** With a real subscribed calendar, **the polling cadence belongs to the subscriber (Google/Apple), not to us** — Google re-fetches on its own schedule, often only every several hours, and **no button on our side can force it.** So either the prior version's button re-fetched **within the app's own view** (achievable, and probably what she remembers), or it appeared to refresh the subscription and could not have. **Establish which before promising the behaviour**, or this ships as a button that looks broken.

**✅ Worth keeping — the rewrite improved three things; don't lose them in a "revert":** `SUMMARY` is now `T1 vs T2` instead of the job name on every row; `UID` is properly qualified (`tsic-game-{Gid}@teamsportsinfo.com`) rather than a bare id; and `VERSION` is **2.0**, which is correct for iCalendar — **legacy's `VERSION:1.0` was simply wrong.** Ann's word *"reverting"* should mean **restoring the Details field, not restoring the legacy file.**
- **Severity**: 🟡 Parts 1 and 2 are user-visible calendar defects; part 3 is an enhancement · all app-side, with a nameable backend regression behind part 1
- **Status**: 🔴 OPEN — for Todd. Filed 08-26. **Part 2 needs a device test, not a code read. Part 3 needs the subscription's actual source located first.**

### AR-048: [TSIC-Teams] Team names overflow the team-name badge — wrap it, and do NOT switch name columns
- **Topic**: **TSIC-Teams** mobile app → team-name badge
- **Request (Ann, 08-26)**: *"Take a look at YJ MidAtlantic on TSIC Teams. The team names can't fit into the team name badge. For example YJ MAA 2031 Great Falls Whelan (how to address this?)"*

**✅ MEASURED — the exact job, the exact names, so the badge can be sized to real data.** The job is **`Yellow Jackets Mid-Atlantic:Players 2025-2026`** — the **only** job in the estate with `bEnableTSICTeams = 1`. It has **10 teams**:
| Len | Team |
|--:|:--|
| **30** | `YJ MA 2031/2032 Fairfax Forand` ← **longest** |
| 29 | `YJ MA 2031 Great Falls Whelan` ← **Ann's example** |
| 28 | `YJMA 2034/2035 Futures Mayer` · `YJ MA 2030 VA Trullender/Lee` |
| 25 | `YJ MA 2034 Counts/Wilmont` |
| 22 | `YJ MA 2029 DMV Obregon` · `YJ MA 2030 DMV Martire` |
| 16-17 | `YJ MA 2033 Harmon` · `YJ MA 2028 Hager` · `YJ MA 2027 Ophof` |
- **So the badge must accommodate ~30 characters**, average 23. **This is 10 teams on one job — a small, fully-known set, not an open-ended problem.**

**🔴 THE OBVIOUS FIX IS A TRAP — do NOT point the badge at a different name column.** `Leagues.teams` carries four (`teamName`, `DisplayName`, `teamFullName`, `OldTeamName`), and **`teamName` is already the SHORTEST.** Estate-wide averages:
| Column | Avg length | What it actually is |
|:--|--:|:--|
| **`teamName`** | **15** | ← already in use, the short one |
| `DisplayName` | 26 | a **`{Club}:{TeamName}` composite** — e.g. `x-Treme Lax Factory:X-Treme Lax Factory Elite 2026` (50 chars for a 30-char team) |
| `teamFullName` | 37 | longer still |
- **Switching to `DisplayName` would nearly double the overflow.** ⚠ And it is **NULL on all 10** of these teams anyway (though set on 23,713 of 51,424 estate-wide), so it would also render blank here. **Two independent reasons not to.**

**🎯 RECOMMENDED: wrap, don't truncate — and there is a house precedent to copy.** The web app hit this exact problem and settled it in **`2508b22b7` — *"long names wrap in the tree and the teams grid instead of ellipsizing"***. **The same answer belongs in the app**: let the badge run to two lines (or shrink the type) rather than clipping.
- ⚠ **Ellipsis is the wrong answer here specifically.** These names end in the **coach surname**, which is the part that distinguishes `YJ MA 2031 Great Falls Whelan` from every other 2031 team. **Truncating from the right destroys exactly the distinguishing token.** Ann has now pushed back on clipped/scrolled content three times (AR-006, AR-016, and the LADT fix above) — the house style is settled: **content wraps.**
- 🔸 **Secondary, if two lines still won't fit**: inside a single club's own app the **`YJ MA` prefix is redundant — all 10 teams carry it** and the user is already inside that club. Dropping it takes the longest to **24** and Ann's example to **23**. ⚠ Fragile as a rule (one team is `YJMA`, no space), so treat it as a display tweak, not a data change.
- 🔸 **Zero-code option worth offering the client**: these are only 10 names and **YJ can rename them today** — no release required. Worth mentioning to Chelsea alongside whatever gets built.

**⚠ CONSTRAINTS:** the app is **not in this repo** (`reference/TSIC-TEAMS-2025` is empty — see AR-046/AR-047), so this is Todd's, edited elsewhere. And ⚠ **dev is a stale prod backup** — the "only one job has the app enabled" finding should be re-checked against production before it is relied on.
- **Severity**: 🟢 Display/layout on a known 10-team set — but the wrong fix (column switch or ellipsis) actively makes it worse
- **Status**: 🔴 OPEN — for Todd. Filed 08-26. **Third of Ann's TSIC-Teams items.**

### AR-038: 🚨🚨 [Privacy / Med Forms] A player's medical form is visible on EVERY registration they hold — across jobs AND across unrelated customers
- **Topic**: Search Registrations → registrant fly-in → **"Medical form on file" / View** (`MedFormController`, `MedFormService`)
- **Observation (Ann, 08-25)**: on **STEPS Elite AIM:Girls Elite Players 2026-2027** she opened **Louisa DeSmedt** and found *"Medical form on file"* with a working **View** button — a form the family uploaded for **UM:Maryland Lacrosse Camps Summer 2026**, a different customer entirely. Her question: *"Will this form display on any registration this player has? It should NOT!"*
- **✅ ANSWER: YES — every registration, on every job, for every customer. Confirmed in code and quantified in data.**
  - **The file is stored ONE PER PLAYER, with no job or registration dimension at all.** `MedFormService.cs` keys everything on `GetFilePath(playerUserId)` — `Exists(playerUserId)`, `ReadAsync(playerUserId)`, `UploadAsync(playerUserId, …)`. Nothing in the path or the lookup carries a job.
  - **Visibility is driven by FILE EXISTENCE, not by the registration.** `medform-view.component.ts` HEADs `/files/medform/by-registration/{registrationId}`; the controller resolves that registration to its `playerUserId` and then answers `_medForms.Exists(playerUserId)` (`MedFormController.cs:162-172`). **So the panel appears wherever the player has a file — regardless of which registration is on screen.**
  - **That is why Ann sees it despite the data saying otherwise**: `Jobs.Registrations.bUploadedMedForm` is **per registration** and reads **0 on the STEPS registration** and **1 on the UM one** — and **the flag is never consulted for display**. The system already records where the form was actually uploaded, and ignores it.
  - **The authorization check is the wrong check.** `ResolveRegistrationAccessAsync` confirms the caller may see **that registration** — correct as far as it goes — and then hands back a file that belongs to a **different job and a different customer**. Nothing verifies the form relates to the job the admin is standing in.
- **📊 SCALE (dev, measured 08-25) — this is not an edge case:**
  - **3,131 players hold a medical form.**
  - **2,316 of them are registered under 2 or more DIFFERENT CUSTOMERS** — so their medical document is reachable by admins at unrelated organisations.
  - 2,462 are visible across 2+ jobs.
  - **Worst case: one player's form is exposed across 37 different customers.**
- **⚠ TREAT THIS AS A DATA-PROTECTION MATTER, NOT A UI BUG.** It is medical information about minors, disclosed across customer boundaries, with no consent path — the family gave it to one camp. **This ranks above every other open item on this list.**
- **For Todd — the decision is what a med form IS, and that has to be settled before the fix:**
  1. **Per-registration document** (what `bUploadedMedForm` already implies): store and serve it keyed by job/registration. Correct and unambiguous; means a family re-uploads per event, which may be exactly right for medical currency.
  2. **Per-player document, scoped at read** (cheapest correct-ish): keep one file, but only serve it where **that registration's own `bUploadedMedForm` is set** — the flag exists and is already accurate. **⚠ Weaker: it is a display gate over a shared file, so any future caller that forgets the flag re-opens the hole.**
    - ### ⛔ CORRECTED 2026-08-25 — **"the flag exists and is already accurate" IS WRONG, and option 2 was therefore UNSAFE.**
      `bUploadedMedForm` is stamped at **row creation** from bare disk existence (`PlayerRegistrationService`, both new-row sites) — so **any registration created after the player uploads gets flagged `1`, whatever job it belongs to.** It read `0` on Ann's STEPS row only because that row predates the UM upload. **Measured: 27 rows already carry `1` in jobs that do not collect med forms.** Accurate in ~99.3% of rows today, structurally unreliable, and degrading. Gating on it would have shipped a control that quietly rots. **Never re-propose option 2.**
  3. **Per-player by design** — if a family genuinely uploads once and it should follow the player, then **cross-CUSTOMER visibility still needs an explicit boundary**, and arguably consent. *"It follows the player"* does not justify a different company's Director reading it.
  - ~~**Recommend 1 or 2 now, and 3 only as a deliberate product decision with consent language.**~~ **SUPERSEDED 08-25 — see the ruling below. Option 2 is unsafe (correction above); the shipped fix is a fourth option neither listed nor considered here: keep per-player storage, gate ACCESS on the event's profile.**
- **✅ ONE PIECE OF GOOD NEWS — access appears to be auditable.** The controller calls `LogAccess("download(reg)", …)` / `LogAccess("download", …)` on every read, allowed or denied. **So it should be possible to establish whether any cross-customer form has actually been opened** rather than merely being openable. **Do that early** — it changes whether this is a latent exposure or a live incident, and that distinction drives what has to be disclosed to whom.
- **✅ RULING + FIX (Todd, 08-25) — GATE ON THE EVENT, DO NOT RE-KEY STORAGE. Built and pushed `1fd88b69`.**
  - **The ruling**: *"Let's make it property of the event, way to scary for director of another event seeing and saying wtf????"* — and on re-upload: *"that is clearly showing parent they own their data and can overwrite with a different form if needed."* So the file stays **one per player** and the **access decision** becomes per event.
  - **Option 1 (re-key storage to `MedForms\{jobId}\{userId}.pdf`) was proposed and REJECTED as over-built** once the data came in: **11 of 1096 jobs collect med forms and all 11 belong to ONE customer (UM/Maryland Lacrosse Camps 2021-2026)**. The gate closes every cross-customer path; re-keying would only have stopped UM-2026 seeing UM-2025, at the cost of a migration with **unrecoverable job attribution** for existing files. **Do not re-propose per-job storage or a migration.**
  - **What shipped**: `TSIC.Domain/Uploads/UploadedDocumentPolicy.cs` — one predicate over `Jobs.PlayerProfileMetadataJson`, matching a field by `name` or `dbColumn`, **failing closed**. Shared by the authorization path and the registration write path so the two cannot drift.
    - `MedFormController.ResolveRegistrationAccessAsync` refuses **before the file is resolved**, returning the same unauthorized shape as a wrong-job hit. **Binds Superuser too** — collecting med forms is a property of the JOB, not of the caller's role.
    - `PlayerRegistrationService` no longer stamps `bUploadedMedForm` true off a file belonging to a different event (fixes the drift described in the correction above, going forward).
    - **No frontend change, no regen** — the View control already renders only on `HEAD` 204, which now returns 403 for non-collecting events, so it simply stops appearing.
      - ### ⛔ CORRECTED 2026-08-26 — **"it simply stops appearing" was true of the CONTROL and false of the SCREEN.** Refusing with **403** made the refusal *loud*: the interceptor toasted *"You do not have permission to access this resource"* over the probe (its `skipErrorToast()` was being ignored — see the OPEN block at the top of this file), so every player fly-in on a non-collecting job threw a red danger toast at the admin. **1,087 of 1,098 jobs.** Now **404** — see the follow-up bullet below.
  - **↳ FOLLOW-UP FIXES (Todd, 08-26) — two, both from the gate meeting surfaces it did not cover.**
    - **`21e066f1` — the gate said 403 where it meant 404.** "This event collects no med form" is the state of nearly every job and is a property of the **event**, not a judgement about the caller; answering 403 made the ordinary case an error and surfaced a permission toast to admins. `ResolveRegistrationAccessAsync` now returns a three-state `RegistrationAccess` — `Allowed` / `NotCollected` (→ **404**, indistinguishable from "no file on disk") / `Forbidden` (→ **403**, still covering wrong role, wrong job, no such registration). Same commit fixes `auth.interceptor.ts`, whose 403 branch ran ahead of the `SKIP_GLOBAL_ERROR_TOAST` check and so silently voided the opt-out for **all 16** `skipErrorToast()` callers. **Nothing refused before is permitted now.**
    - **`eaaffd09` — the check-in screen was still leaking, and AR-038 never covered it.** `CheckinRepository` set `HasMedForm = r.BUploadedMedForm ?? false` with **no event gate** — the *View* action went through the gated controller, but the *indicator* did not, so it announced *"this child has a medical form on file"* to directors who never collected one. Measured 08-26: **48 rows across 10 unrelated customers** (Live Love Lax 18, LI Elite 10, American Select 4, StateOne 4, ODU 3, Premier 2, Signature Sports 2, UM Prospect Clinics 2, STEPS 1, LFTC 1, AIM 1). Now AND'd against the same `UploadedDocumentPolicy` predicate, resolved once per call off the team's job. *(This supersedes the "27 stale `1` rows — harmless now" note below: they were not harmless, because a second surface read them.)*
    - **⛔ A `customerId` gate was authorised by Todd 08-26 and DELIBERATELY NOT TAKEN — do not build it.** Corroboration ran and passed: all **11** collecting jobs belong to customer `502F0C58-C643-43DA-9BFE-00E140F1A19B` (**UM**), zero unmatched, and **3,132 of 3,133** flag-holders also registered at UM. It was declined anyway because it **selects the identical 11 jobs** the metadata predicate already selects, and the one thing it could have added — another customer self-granting the flag — **is already impossible**: `PlayerProfileMetadataJson` is **SuperUser-only on both read and write** (`JobConfigService.cs:339` / `:811`). Todd's *"I will not offer this to any other customer"* is therefore already enforced by the fact that **only he can turn it on**. A hardcoded customer GUID would add a constant no data change keeps honest, with cross-customer SU migration Phase 4 still unfinished. ⚠ **Not corroborated: the file store.** This box's `RegFileUploads\MedForms` holds **0 files** (statics live on PHOENIX), so "all forms originate at UM" rests on DB flags only. One flag-holder (`8196df96-…`, StateOne C&C 2026) has no UM registration at all — moot, since StateOne does not collect and they are hidden under either design.
  - **Vaccine cards checked at the same time: 0 jobs collect `bUploadedVaccineCard` and this stack has NO consumer for it** — no upload, no view, no service. Legacy-only, and legacy's statics URL is already 404'd. A `VaccineCardField` constant sits in the policy so any future reader inherits the gate by construction.
  - **Left deliberately undone, needs its own ruling**: upload/delete still do not refresh `bUploadedMedForm` on existing rows, so it keeps drifting both ways. Also the **27 stale `1` rows** in non-collecting jobs — harmless now (the gate ignores the flag) but a data-only cleanup if wanted.
  - Tests: `dotnet test --filter "FullyQualifiedName~PlayerMedFormStampTests"` (3; the 2 originals mocked `PlayerProfileMetadataJson = null` and needed a collecting profile).
- **Severity**: 🔴🔴 **CRITICAL — cross-customer disclosure of minors' medical data.** No exploit needed: any admin authorised on any job the player has ever registered for can open it in two clicks.
- **✅ FIX VERIFIED (Ann, 08-26)** — she re-checked **Clara Scheets on All American Aim** and the control is gone. Re-measured against the dev DB the same day, so the closure is quantified rather than argued:
  | Measure | Value |
  |:--|--:|
  | Jobs collecting a med form (of 1,095) | **11 — all ONE customer** |
  | Players holding a form | 3,131 |
  | Their registrations, total | 28,212 |
  | …on a **non-collecting** job → **now refused** | **23,610 (84%)** |
  | **Customers able to reach a given player's form** | **52 → 1** |
  | Clara Scheets specifically | **21 regs / 7 customers → 1 collecting job** |
- **↳ Ann's follow-up: a *"You do not have permission to access this resource"* toast seen once on 08-26, not reproducible. ✅ ANSWERED — not a defect, and it will not surface on other sites.**
  - **Opening a fly-in cannot raise it.** The existence probe passes `skipErrorToast()` and has since the component was first written (`1bee809e2`, `medform-view.component.ts:86-92`) — on a blocked event the control simply stays hidden, silently. That is why she cannot reproduce it.
  - **The only med-form path that can toast is clicking View** (`:98` — the GET is *not* toast-suppressed, unlike the probe). Because both routes share one `ResolveRegistrationAccessAsync`, they cannot disagree; the button can only be shown-then-denied **across a backend restart** — probe answers under the old code, click lands on the new. Transient, cleared by a refresh, which matches her account exactly.
  - ⚠ **Cannot be attributed with certainty**: that string is the app's **generic** 403 (`auth.interceptor.ts:50-53`), fired for every endpoint, and dev's `logs.AppLog` is empty — so there is no trail proving it came from med forms at all. Recorded as reasoning, not as a confirmed med-form denial.
- **Status**: 🟢 **CODE FIXED + PUSHED 08-25** (`1fd88b69`), **✅ FIX VERIFIED BY ANN 08-26.** **⚠ STILL NOT CLOSED as an item: the `LogAccess` review (opened vs merely openable) is outstanding and is the half that decides disclosure.** Filed 08-25 from Ann's observation on `stepseliteaim-players-2026-2027` (Louisa DeSmedt; form uploaded under `um-summercamps-2026`).

### AR-037: [ARB / X-Job] SuperUser option to email ALL expiring credit cards ACROSS jobs, not one job at a time
- **Topic**: ARB Subscription Health → **Expiring Cards** → a cross-job SuperUser send
- **Request (Ann, 08-25)**: an option for **SuperUser to send to ALL expiring CCs across jobs**, rather than working job by job.
- **⚠ FILING NOTE: this was discussed previously but was NEVER RECORDED.** Searched every punchlist (`After-Release`, `Admin-Menus`, `Payment-Test`, and the rest) — no item exists for it. **Recording it now so it stops being re-remembered and re-lost.** If an older ruling on it turns up, reconcile rather than assuming this entry is the whole history.
- **Today it is strictly per-job.** ARB Health's Expiring Cards lookup runs against **one job's** subscriptions, and the *"Credit Card Expiration Notice"* template (`arb-health.component.ts:61-77`) is sent from that job's screen. A SuperUser wanting every expiring card must open every job in turn.
- **🔴 THE CONSTRAINT THAT SHAPES THE WHOLE FEATURE — Expiring Cards is a LIVE PRODUCTION Authorize.net call, not a database read.** `ArbDefensiveService.cs:56-63`: *"FORCED PRODUCTION — real subscriptions live only on the production account, so the sandbox list is always empty off-Production"*, then `ARBGetSubscriptionListRequest` using **`GetJobAdnProductionCredentials_FromJobId(jobId)`**.
  - ~~**Credentials are per job**, so a cross-job sweep is **one live ADN API call per job** — not one query. Across a few hundred live jobs that is a few hundred production calls to a payment processor, with rate limits, latency and partial-failure to design for.~~
  - ### ✅ CORRECTED 2026-08-25 — **THE COST ARGUMENT ABOVE IS WRONG BY TWO ORDERS OF MAGNITUDE.**
    Credentials resolve per **Customer** (`Jobs.Customers.adnLoginID`), not per job, and `ARBGetSubscriptionList` returns the **whole merchant account's** list regardless of who asks. Measured on production:
    **21 live jobs carry ARB plans · 11 customers behind them · `1` distinct merchant account** (`teamspt52`). Estate-wide there are only two accounts (`teamspt52` 14,480 subs, `3MGs4r72` 490).
    **ONE API call covers every live ARB job.** Corollary: the current per-job screen already pulls the entire estate and discards all but one job's worth on every click — **a scheduled estate-wide sweep is CHEAPER than what happens today**, not more expensive. The infeasibility that shaped this item was an accounting error.
    - **⚠ But one real blocker before anything runs unattended:** `AdnApiService.ARBGetSubscriptionListRequest` (~:452) ends in `catch { return null; }`, and null is indistinguishable from "no results". Clicked by a human that is a shrug; **on a timer a dead ADN call renders as "no expiring cards" — green all-clear, no mail, nobody told.** Silent, and failing in the direction that hides the problem.
    - **⚠ And a latent one our change would activate:** `StartDefensiveEmailsAsync` builds one `notifiedNames` list and gives it to every director. Correct today (single-job scope); **estate-wide it would send every director every club's families.** Needs partitioning by job — requires `JobId` on `ArbFlaggedRegistrantDto` (projection has it, `MapToDto` drops it) ⇒ regen API models.
  - **Contrast the sibling lookup**: "Behind in Payment" is a *pure DB read* off stored status (`:85-87`). **The expensive one is exactly the one Ann wants to run across everything.**
  - **⚠ PRECEDENT, and it points the other way**: under **PL-055** this lookup was deliberately made **click-only** — it used to auto-fire on page open, and that was judged wrong precisely *because* it is a live production query. A cross-job "send to all" must not casually undo that reasoning. **Whatever is built should make the cost visible and deliberate** (an explicit run, a progress/partial-failure report, and probably a cached result rather than a live sweep per send).
- **For Todd — decisions before any build:**
  1. **Sweep or cache?** A nightly/periodic sweep storing expiry data would make the cross-job view a DB read, and is likely the only sane basis for "all jobs". A live sweep on demand puts a few hundred ADN calls behind one button.
  2. **Scope**: all jobs, or only jobs whose user window is still open? An expired job's families have no reason to update a card.
  3. **⚠ DEDUPE ACROSS JOBS.** A parent with players in several jobs will match in each one. Sending per job means the same family gets several near-identical notices in one run. **Decide per-family or per-registration before building** — this is the difference between a helpful sweep and a complaint.
  4. **Whose name is on it?** Per-job sends carry that job's From/display identity (`Jobs.DisplayName`, see AR-036). A cross-job blast from a SuperUser has to decide whether each mail still looks like it came from the club, or from TSIC.
  5. **Email Log**: sends must land in `Jobs.emailLogs` per job, as with every other send — the same requirement Ann set on AR-021.
- **🔴 ANN'S SECOND PASS, 08-25 — SHE IS RE-RAISING THIS DELIBERATELY, AFTER REVIEWING IT WITH CHELSEA.** *"Note Todd has said he wouldn't do this so I am bringing it up again after reviewing with Chelsea."* **Treat it as a considered re-raise, not a forgotten ask.** Four things she added, and the third changes the severity:
  1. **Placement + permission**: *"it could be in any ARB Health but only viewable by SuperUsers."* So — no new screen needed, and no exposure to Directors. **That answers the obvious objection to a bulk send: the blast button is not being handed to customers.**
  2. **⚠ SCOPE IS EVERY JOB — corrected by Ann 08-25: "YES ALL JOBS!!!!"** Claude first read *"the linked Players site ONLY"* as limiting the **sweep**; it limits the **recipients**. **The sweep covers ALL jobs.** For each expiring card found, the notice goes to the users **and the Directors of that subscription's own linked Players site** — *"ONLY"* means do not copy Directors of unrelated jobs. **Do not narrow the sweep on the strength of that word.**
     - **So the full cost stands**: every job with ARB plans, one **live forced-production ADN call each** (`ArbDefensiveService.cs:56-63`). **That is precisely why her scheduled option below is the answer rather than a nice-to-have** — an operator cannot sit through an estate-wide live sweep, but a timer can.
     - **Recipient resolution is its own small design**: given a subscription, resolve **which Players site it belongs to**, then that site's Directors. Getting this wrong sends a club's payment problem to another club's Directors.
  3. **⚠ THE RELIABILITY ARGUMENT — this is the part that makes it more than convenience.** *"Chelsea has been doing this routinely, not the Directors and this is a reliability factor. If they do not update and another payment goes in and it is declined, it can lead to a General Error with ADN which isn't swept/imported. This is infrequent but a real issue."*
     - **So the current state is a person remembering to do it, job by job** — TSIC staff, not the customer. Manual, per-job, and dependent on one individual's diligence.
     - **And the failure mode is not just "a payment fails".** A declined draft against an expired card can produce an **ADN General Error**, and **that state is not picked up by the nightly sweep/import** — so the record silently diverges from what ADN actually holds. **That connects this item to AR-013/AR-027 territory**: stale or wrong ARB status is exactly what produced the hidden Pay Balance Due row and the "Update Failed" screen that had already charged. **Prevention is cheaper than reconciling after the fact.**
     - **⚠ Whoever picks this up should confirm the General Error path** — what ADN returns, why the sweep misses it, and whether it needs its own handling regardless of this feature. **That may be a defect hiding behind a feature request.**
  - ### ✅ CONFIRMED 2026-08-25 (Todd's session) — **THE SWEEP DOES IMPORT THEM. The premise is false.**
    Verified against source, the ADN SDK, and the production DB. **Do not re-derive the old assumption.**
    - **Authorize.Net puts declines and errors INSIDE settled batches** — `batchStatisticType` carries `declineCount` and `errorCount` (reflected from `AuthorizeNet.dll` 2.0.4). So `GetTransactionList_ByBatchId` returns them, and `IsArbCandidate` (`AdnSweepService.cs:367`) has always accepted `settledSuccessfully | declined | generalError`. Legacy did the same.
    - **Sweep-written rows in `Jobs.Registration_Accounting`** (`Payamt = 0`, `LebUserId` = SuperUser, `Createdate` at 04:00:xx): **3,832 `declined`** (2020-07-25 → 2026-08-24) and **119 `generalError`** (2020-08-26 → 2026-05-01). Post-golive: 936 settled + **50 declined**, 1–10 every morning.
    - **The import is correct in every respect** — $0 audit row, totals untouched, subscription status synced from ADN. **Records do NOT diverge. The ledger is accurate.**
    - `generalError` has not fired since **2026-05-01, which is pre-golive** — so anything Chelsea saw was on legacy. Same code path as `declined`; a matter of which flavour occurred, not of coverage.
