- **Parts 2 and 3 deliberately NOT built — Todd's ruling that part 1 is the whole answer.** No Team-level ⓘ, no Active-vs-Self-Roster gloss; the Team grid already shows both columns side by side. **h-scroll ACCEPTED (Todd, 08-19): "3rd party pushing off is perfectly fine"** — Ann's no-h-scroll constraint is overridden, no donor column was shrunk (M/F and 3rd Party are AM-038-tuned; `Fees` at 220px is the slack if it's ever revisited).
- **Status**: ✅ VERIFIED PASSING (Ann, 08-21) — **CLOSED.** SELF ROSTER column confirmed on the Age Group grid; deploy evidently landed (she saw it). Parts 2–3 stay unbuilt per Todd's 08-19 ruling, and the h-scroll override stands — both acknowledged in the same pass.

### AR-005: [Coach Registrations / Security] SuperUser login was able to self-register as a Coach — intended?
- **Topic**: Coach Registrations → role-lane purity
- **Observation (Ann)**: Using the **TSIC SuperUser login**, Ann was able to **self-register as a Coach** on a job. Flagging because it was unexpected.
- **Question for Todd**: Is this intended? Two angles:
  1. **Testing convenience** — SuperUser is deliberately exempt from most gates (cross-job ops), so being able to register-as-anything may be by design for testing.
  2. **Lane-purity risk** — but a real Coach/Staff registration on a SuperUser account writes a live role grant. Per the AM-004 lane model, only LIVE registrations classify an account's eligibility; a stray Staff row on the SuperUser account could muddy that, or surface the SuperUser in coach/roster views. Confirm this doesn't poison the SuperUser's lane or leak it into director-facing lists.
- **For Todd**: decide whether SuperUser should be **blocked** from self-registering as a Coach (clean-lane), or **allowed** (testing) — and if allowed, confirm the resulting Staff row is filtered out of coach/roster/approval surfaces the way other admin lanes are.
- **Severity**: Question / possible security-lane concern (non-blocking; verify before go-live if it affects real accounts)
- **CONFIRMED TRUE (Claude, 08-19) — the behavior is real, no guard exists.** `POST /api/adult-registration/register-existing` is `[Authorize]` and nothing more (`AdultRegistrationController.cs:105`) — no role restriction, no admin-account exclusion. The role is resolved purely from job type (`AdultRegistrationService.ResolveCoach`, :1584) and real Registration rows are written against the SuperUser's own user id: an **UnassignedAdult** row on a Club site, or **one Staff row per team** (on the real roster, with the per-team fee cascade stamped) on Tournament/League.
- **NOT a security problem — ruled (Todd, 08-19): "why do we need to do anything?"** The direction is **downward**: reaching this endpoint requires already being authenticated as SuperUser, an account that already exceeds everything Staff grants. **No privilege escalation, no attacker, no customer-visible exposure.** What it actually produces is lane pollution with a money artifact — SuperUser visible in a director's Coach Approvals queue (the queue query filters role + active only, no admin exclusion), on a live team roster, holding a "Staff" login option, and carrying a coach fee. Every one of those is self-inflicted by TSIC staff, rare, and reversible by hand (deny the row, delete the roster row, void the fee). A guard would be new code on a live production auth path defending against our own testing. **WILL NOT DO. Do not re-propose the SuperUser block.**
- **Where the real exposure sits, if it is ever revisited — and it holds:** on Tournament/League with coach registration open, **any member of the public can self-place as Staff on ANY team in the event** — `BuildAndAddRegistrationsAsync` validates only that the team belongs to the job, never that the registrant has any relationship to it, and anonymous `RegisterNewUser` reaches the same code. That is the deliberate 2026-08-14 two-regime design (the club vouches; the director can't vet strangers). Verified the control is real: `MyRosterService.cs:37-63` gates on **`BAllowRosterViewAdult`** (default false, clone-reset to false, consent-gated in the Teams tab), restricts to the caller's **own** `AssignedTeamId`, and refuses system-bucket teams. **Trigger to re-examine: a live job running with `BAllowRosterViewAdult = true`.** Nothing else here warrants attention.
- **Status**: ✅ CLOSED — Won't Fix (Todd, 08-19). Behavior confirmed real; not a security issue; no guard added. ✅ Acknowledged (Ann, 08-21).

### AR-004: [Rosters / PDF] Roster PDF truncates the Position column — "Midfield" prints as "Midfiel"
- **Topic**: Rosters → PDF export → Position column
- **Observation (Ann)**: In the generated **roster PDF**, the **Position** value is **cut off** — e.g. "Midfield" renders as "Midfiel" (last character clipped). The column is too narrow / the cell text is being truncated rather than fit.
- **For Todd**: widen the Position column (or shrink/auto-fit the text) in the roster PDF generator so full position names print. Check the longest expected values (Midfield, Attack, Defense, Goalie, Long Stick Midfield / LSM, etc.) so the fix holds for the widest label, not just "Midfield". Likely a fixed column width or a text-clip in the PDF layout, not a data problem (the stored value is complete).
- **Severity**: Bug / output correctness (non-blocking; cosmetic-but-wrong on a printed artifact)
- **BLOCKED — WE DO NOT KNOW WHICH REPORT (Todd, 08-17): "not approachable until you know which report, you could really cause problems with packed reports."** Do NOT touch a generator on inference. **NEXT STEP: ask Ann which roster export she ran.**
- **Investigation banked (Claude, 08-17) — four generators have a Position column, each with its own width mechanism:**
  - `PackedRosterPdfService` — label literally **"Position"**, proportional `DefaultWidthWeight = 32`, `SupportsLongText = false` (:49). **HIGHEST RISK to change** — dense legacy-parity export family (PackedByPosition / by-School siblings); widening steals width from neighbours across every variant.
  - `MyRosterPdfService` — header **"Pos"**, fixed `30f` (:95). Would clip far harder than one character.
  - `ClubRosterPdfService` — no Position column at all; `DrawClip`s Position onto line 2 of the **DOB** column's width (:258).
  - `RosterTablePdfService` — configurable `"position"` key (:346); width comes from the caller's column set.
  - **None of them measure the text — they all clip.** Symptom of exactly one character lost out of eight *suggests* Packed Roster, but that is inference from a screenshot, not proof, and it is precisely the one that must not be changed on a guess.
  - **`SupportsLongText = false` is a deliberate flag, not an oversight** — so "widen" and "wrap" are two genuinely different fixes (wrap = variable row heights on a dense report).
  - **Size to the longest value actually in the data, not to "Midfield"** — `Long Stick Midfield` is far wider, so a fix eyeballed against Midfield leaves the bug live for LSM. Same approach settled in AM-038 (size to the longest real value, don't eyeball).
- **✅ UN-BLOCKED — REPORT IDENTIFIED (Ann + Claude, 08-21). It is `MyRosterPdfService`, NOT Packed Roster.**
  - **Ann's location**: "the roster that Coach has access to once approved" → the teammate roster card view (Find a teammate… / **Download PDF** / Email Selected / Email All) → **Download PDF**. Caller chain confirmed: `MyRosterService` → `IMyRosterPdfService` (`Program.cs:388`).
  - **Proof, not inference — three independent matches** against Ann's PDF screenshot:
    1. Header reads **`Pos`**, not `Position`. Packed Roster labels it `Position` in full; only `MyRosterPdfService` uses the abbreviation (`:95`).
    2. Column set is byte-for-byte `BuildColumns()` (`:91-101`): `# · Player · Pos · Grad · Email · Phone · Contact 1 · Contact 2`.
    3. Clip lengths fit the 30f width exactly — `midfield`(8)→`midfiel`, `defense`(7)→`defens`, `attack`(6) intact.
  - **⚠️ CORRECTION to the 08-17 banked investigation**: it reasoned "one character out of eight *suggests* Packed Roster." **That inference was wrong.** Packed Roster needs no change for this bug, and Todd's refusal to act on the guess is exactly what prevented a needless edit to the highest-risk generator. Leave `PackedRosterPdfService` alone.
- **Target (single line)**: `MyRosterPdfService.cs:95` — `new Col("Pos", 30f, PdfTextAlignment.Center, false, …)`. The `false` is `Wrap`, so the cell clips rather than fits.
  - **Widths are proportional weights, normalized** (`:37-38`, `c.Width / sumW * contentW`). Sum = 734 over a landscape-Letter 0.4in content width (~734pt), so today the weights ≈ points 1:1 — but **any increase to `Pos` shrinks every other column**, it is not free space.
  - **Donor columns**: `Contact 1` and `Contact 2` at **157f each** are by far the fattest and both already `Wrap: true`, so they absorb a trim by growing taller, not by clipping. `Email` (138f) is the next candidate. Do **not** raid `#`(26f) / `Grad`(34f) / `Phone`(72f).
  - **Widen vs wrap**: unlike Packed Roster, this report has **no `SupportsLongText = false` constraint** — `MeasureRowHeight` (`:156-170`) already computes per-row height from wrapped cells, so flipping `Pos` to `Wrap: true` is genuinely available here and costs only row height on long values.
- **⚠ STILL REQUIRED before sizing — query the real data.** Ann's sample tops out at `midfield` (8). **Do NOT size to that.** `Long Stick Midfield` (19) would still clip at any width that merely fits "midfield". Pull `SELECT DISTINCT Position` across live registrations, size to the longest actual value (or wrap), per the AM-038 precedent already cited above. Stored values appear **lowercase** in the PDF (`midfiel`, `attack`, `defens`) — confirm whether that is the stored casing or a render-time transform.
- **✅ DATA QUERY DONE 08-23 — it changes the sizing.** `Jobs.Registrations.position` is FREE TEXT. Distinct values by length: `still figuring it out` (21 chars, 23 rows) · `not yet determined` (18, 1) · `not sure yet` (12, 21) · `unknown` (7, 355) · then the real ones, all ≤8 — `midfield` 226,708 · `attack` 172,346 · `defense` 147,334 · `goalie` 45,403 · `forward` 380 · `LSM` 150.
  - **⚠ `Long Stick Midfield` DOES NOT EXIST in the data — it is stored as `LSM` (3 chars).** The 08-21 instruction to size against it was chasing a value that is not there.
  - **The longest values are junk data entry, not positions.** Widening to fit `still figuring it out` would cost ~20pt of table width for ~45 rows out of ~590k. **Wrap handles them; width should not.**
  - **Casing is genuinely stored lowercase** (`midfield`, `attack`), with `LSM` / `F/O` uppercase — mixed, not a render-time transform. No casing change made.
  - **Widest value that must FIT on one line is `unknown` at 32.0pt** in Helvetica 8 — wider than `midfield` (27.6) or `defense` (28.5).
- **✅ ARITHMETIC CONFIRMS ANN'S EXACT SYMPTOM, so the target was right**: `Pos` 30f normalizes to 30.0pt (weights sum 734 against a 734.4pt content area, ~1:1), leaving **24.0pt** usable after `CellPadX` * 2. `midfield` measures 27.6pt → clips; `midfiel` is 23.1pt → fits. `defense` 28.5 → `defens`. Not inference.
- **Status**: 🟡 **BUILT (Todd, 08-23) — `MyRosterPdfService.cs:106`.** `Pos` **30f → 40f** (34pt usable, fits `unknown` with headroom) **and `Wrap: false` → `true`** for the free-text outliers. The 10f is paid for by `Contact 2` **157f → 147f**, which already wraps and so absorbs it by growing taller rather than clipping. `MeasureRowHeight` (`:156-170`) already sizes rows off wrapped cells, so nothing else changed. **`PackedRosterPdfService` untouched, as ruled.** Note `PdfWordWrapType.Word` wraps on spaces, so a hypothetical single unbroken 20-char token would still clip — none exists. ✅ **VERIFIED PASSING (Ann, 08-24) — CLOSED.** Coach → team roster card → Download PDF: positions print in full. Tested against a current dev deploy (Ann confirmed dev up to date 08-24). ORIGINAL: 🔴 OPEN — un-parked 08-21; report identified, target line known, data query outstanding.

### AR-003: [Coach Registrations] "Teams You're Coaching" list is hard to read — stack it vertically with a light (blue?) badge per team
- **Topic**: Coach Registrations → adult registration wizard → "Teams You're Coaching" review section (direct-placement Staff on Tournament/League)
- **Applies to**: **Direct-to-roster** coach type (tournaments, leagues) — the step showing the teams the coach will be placed on, above the "Submitting registers you as staff on **every team listed above**" callout.
- **Request (Ann)**: The teams listed under **"TEAMS YOU'RE COACHING"** are **not easily seen** — they currently run **left-to-right inline** on one line, which reads as a run-on. Make each team stand out:
  1. Render as a **vertical list** (one team per line, not a horizontal left→right row).
  2. Give each team a **light badge** (blue suggested) so they're visually distinct and scannable.
  - See attached image: two teams shown inline (`3 Point:2028:Unassigned:3 Point 2028   214LAXDALLAS:2028:Unassigned:214LAXDALLAS 2028`) with the amber "every team listed above" callout beneath.
- **For Todd**: this is the direct-placement review list (the one gated by the server-derived `DirectPlacement` flag from `e6d3bd08`). Use design-system tokens for the badge (no hardcoded blue — e.g. `.bg-primary-subtle` / a subtle info badge), keep the amber callout as-is.
- **Severity**: UI / readability (non-blocking)
- **FIXED (Claude, 08-17) — awaiting Ann verify.** CSS only, three declarations on `.teams-wrap` in `review-step.component.ts`: `flex-direction: column` + `align-items: flex-start` (each pill hugs its own text rather than stretching the column) + gap `--space-1` → `--space-2`. No template, binding, logic, or colour change.
  - **The badge Ann asked for already existed** — `.review-team-pill` is already primary-tinted background + primary border + primary text, all token-derived. The pills simply weren't stacked: `flex-wrap: wrap` at a 4px gap put two long `Club:Age:Division:Team` labels side by side, which is the run-on she saw. Nothing was added for the badge.
  - **Shape precedent:** the Profile step one screen earlier already renders this same selection as a vertical one-per-line list — Review was the odd one out. Matched its *arrangement*, kept Review's *styling*; did NOT convert to Profile's `<ul>`, which would be a template change and would drop the badge.
  - **Both faces of the section move, intended:** direct-placement "Teams You're Coaching" and the club-site "Teams Requested" `@else` branch share `.teams-wrap`. Both were inline; both now stack.
  - **Risk:** nil. Angular view encapsulation scopes the rule to this component; `.teams-wrap` exists nowhere else (the class *name* `.review-team-pill` is also used by the player review step, but as an independent scoped copy — untouched either way). No colour declarations, so every palette and both light/dark render as before. No animation, so nothing for reduced-motion. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean.
- **NOT DONE, deliberately — the label noise.** `3 Point:2028:Unassigned:3 Point 2028` repeats the club and year and leaks the literal **"Unassigned"** division placeholder into customer-facing text. It comes from `availableTeams().displayText`, the SAME value feeding the ej2 team picker one step earlier. Rejected both fixes: a Review-only transform would print one team under two different names three clicks apart in one wizard (worse than a noisy label), and changing `displayText` at the source moves the picker — not no-risk, and not what Ann filed. **File separately if the placeholder leak matters.**
- **Status**: ✅ VERIFIED PASSING (Ann, 08-21) — **CLOSED.** Stacked one-per-line list confirmed on the Review step. The label-noise follow-up above (`Club:Year:Unassigned:Team` repetition) remains unfiled — raise it as its own item if it matters.

### AR-002: [Coach Registrations] Tournament/league coaches register directly as Staff (never Unassigned Adult) — confirm behavior + clean up residual Unassigned Adults
- **Topic**: Coach Registrations → Staff vs Unassigned Adult
- **Applies to**: **Direct-to-roster** coach type (tournaments, leagues). Ann believes this pertains to the **upcoming Fall tournaments** specifically.
- **Observation (Ann)**: On tournament/league sites, a Coach is registered **directly as Staff** and **never passes through the Unassigned Adult state**.
- **Confirm (for Todd)**: Is this the intended behavior — direct-to-Staff, no Unassigned Adult step?
- **If confirmed, two follow-ups**:
  1. **Director-account creation** — does this mean tournament/league sites **cannot be used to create a Director account** (since a Director account path presumably runs through the Unassigned Adult → elevate flow)? Confirm true/false. *(Ties to AR-001's "register as Coach en route to Director" case — if direct-to-Staff is the rule, that AR-001 rationale may not hold.)*
  2. **Residual cleanup** — there are **leftover Unassigned Adult registrations from before the release** on these jobs. Were they all **approved**? If so, should we **delete the now-distinct Unassigned Adult registrations** so directors aren't confused by duplicate/orphaned adult rows?
- **Severity**: Question + data cleanup (non-blocking; verify before deleting anything)
- **Data check available**: Claude can enumerate the residual Unassigned Adult registrations on the upcoming Fall tournament jobs (count + per-job breakdown) so the cleanup decision is made against real numbers, not an estimate.
- **CONFIRMED (Claude, code + data, 2026-08-14 code):** The direct-to-Staff behavior is **intended and current**. Todd's commit `e6d3bd08` ("two-regime coach registration") makes Tournament/League coach self-reg resolve to **`RoleConstants.Staff`** with **one Staff Registration per selected team** (binding, no approval, no UnassignedAdult row). Club (player-reg) sites keep the UnassignedAdult funnel + Roster Swapper approval. Live data corroborates: Fall 2026 tournaments are Staff-only, zero UnassignedAdult (e.g. `Karenpequa@aol.com` = 7 Staff rows minted in the same instant = one per team).
  - **So follow-up #1 (Director-account path):** on Tournament/League there is no UnassignedAdult step at all, which reinforces AR-001's question — a coach on these sites lands as Staff, not as an elevatable Unassigned Adult. Todd to confirm whether the "register as Coach → become Director" path is expected to exist on team-reg sites (it may only exist on Club sites).
  - **So follow-up #2 (residual cleanup) — two DISTINCT cases:**
    - **Club sites:** already handled — commit `75d82744` **deletes the UnassignedAdult row when a director grants a pending coach**, so no residual accumulates going forward.
    - **Tournament/League sites:** the grant-delete does **not** apply (there's no grant step here — coaches are placed directly). Any UnassignedAdult rows on these jobs are **pre-release residue** from the old universal-funnel behavior, orphaned by the regime switch. **This is the case that still needs a one-time cleanup decision** — enumerate + confirm none are legitimately in-flight, then delete so directors don't see phantom Unassigned Adults next to the real Staff placements.
- **RULING — FULLY RESOLVED, no code change (Todd, 08-19). CLOSED; do not re-raise either half.**
  - **(a) Director-account path — club-site-only, by design.** `AdministratorService.AddAdministratorAsync` accepts exactly two shapes: a **lane-pure admin account** (already Director/SuperDirector somewhere), or **exactly one active Unassigned Adult row on any job of any customer**. A **Staff** row qualifies as neither — so a tournament/league coach registration cannot seed a Director account. The person must register as a coach on a **Club** site (cross-customer is allowed), then be accepted here. Accepted as correct: Staff is a binding roster hat, not a vetting artifact.
  - **Known, accepted consequence — the empty-search copy loops on team-reg sites.** `admin-form-modal.component.ts:151` (`outsideLane`) tells the director *"Register the person as a coach (any event site), then accept them here"*; on a tournament, obeying that mints Staff again, forever. **Only bites a person with no prior admin role anywhere** — an existing Director elsewhere is added normally. Left as-is by ruling; file a fresh item only if a director actually hits it.
  - **(b) Residual UA cleanup — not pursued.** The pre-release Unassigned Adult rows on tournament/league jobs stay where they are. No enumeration was run, nothing deleted. Club side remains covered by `75d82744`.
- **Diagnostic fact worth reusing:** "tournament/league" is **not one bucket** for adult roles. The `unassigned` role key is **rejected outright on Tournament** but **still valid on League** (`AdultRegistrationService.cs:1509`), so a league can legitimately still mint an approval-queue row — even though the app's own links route league coaches to the `coach` key → Staff (`translate-legacy-urls.pipe.ts:213`). Bears directly on AR-001.
- **Status**: ✅ CLOSED (Todd, 08-19) — behavior CONFIRMED and intended; both follow-ups ruled, no work taken. ✅ Acknowledged (Ann, 08-21).

### AR-001: [Coach Registrations] Coach Approval menu still shows for tournaments/leagues (direct-to-roster) — keep or hide?
- **Topic**: Coach Registrations → Coach Approval menu
- **Applies to**: **Direct-to-roster** coach type (tournaments, leagues) — where coaches are placed straight onto the team roster and the approval step isn't used.
- **Observation (Ann)**: The **Coach Approval** menu is still present for tournament/league sites, even though those sites place coaches directly on rosters and won't use the approval workflow.
- **Ann's leaning**: Initially thought we should **hide it** on these site types. But there may be a legitimate reason to keep it: someone could register as a **Coach** on this type of site **as part of the process of being added as a Director** — in which case the approval menu could still be useful.
- **Question for Todd**: Should the Coach Approval menu be **hidden** on direct-to-roster (tournament/league) sites, or **left visible** to support the "register as Coach en route to Director" case? Decision his call.
- **Severity**: Question / product decision (non-blocking)
- **RULING — WILL NOT DO (Todd, 08-19): "ignore it, not a problem."** The menu stays visible on every job type. No nav visibility rule, no code, no Nav Editor change. **Do not re-propose hiding it.**
- **Findings banked (Claude, 08-19), so this isn't re-derived:**
  - Ann's "register as Coach en route to Director" rationale is **dead** — settled by the AR-002 ruling: a tournament/league coach registration mints **Staff**, which never reaches this queue, and the Director path is club-site-only. The menu is NOT being kept for that reason; it's kept because the cost of the empty screen is nil.
  - The screen is **not guaranteed empty** anyway: the queue is every active Unassigned Adult row on the job (`RegistrationRepository.GetUnassignedAdultQueueAsync`, :3152), and the pre-release residue (AR-002b, ruled to stay) still shows there — plus on **League** the `unassigned` key remains a live carve-out. **Tournament** is the only type where the platform categorically refuses to mint a new row.
  - Had we hidden it, it would have been a **data** change, not code: the nav row seeds with `VisibilityRules = NULL` (`scripts/5) Re-Set Nav System.sql:139`) and the engine already supports a `jobTypes` **allowlist** (`visibility-rules.ts:81`), settable by a SuperUser in **Configure → Nav Editor** — the same mechanism Check-In and Camp Groups use. Allowlist-only, so "hide on Tournament" means listing the other five types.
  - **Second door, ungated:** the Roster Swapper header carries its own **Coach Approvals** button with no job-type check (`roster-swapper.component.html:7`). Any future attempt to hide this screen must gate that too, or the door dangles. Moot under this ruling.
- **Status**: ✅ CLOSED — Won't Fix (Todd, 08-19). Menu stays visible everywhere. ✅ Acknowledged (Ann, 08-21).
